Tesla’s FSD v15 turns driving visuals into Grand Theft Auto
Unknown
Unknown Publisher
2026-11-23
14:12:28 UTC
23:12:28 KST

Tesla has begun rolling out Full Self-Driving (Supervised) v15, calling it a ground-up overhaul of its end-to-end driver assist system. The centerpiece is Reality View, a real-time generative display that swaps out old wireframe graphics for a live, overhead camera angle that lets drivers see and tap to influence what the car plans to do next.
Tesla’s FSD v15 turns driving visuals into Grand Theft Auto
The update swaps blocky wireframes for a photorealistic overhead feed and promises a “25x” safety boost, even as federal regulators continue investigating the software.
The third-person perspective renders buildings, crosswalks, pedestrians, and surrounding traffic with a photorealism that has drawn immediate comparisons to Grand Theft Auto VI (which conveniently launched just days earlier). Even Elon Musk couldn’t resist the comparison on X, posting “FSD is GTA irl” shortly after the update dropped on Saturday.
Under the hood, FSD v15 runs on an upgraded vision encoder shared across Tesla’s FSD, Robotaxi, and Optimus humanoid robot programs. By training the model on millions of online videos, Tesla claims its AI learned to prevent serious driving mistakes 25 times more often while using less computing power. Of course, the update is built for vehicles running Tesla’s newer AI4 (HW4) hardware and upcoming Cybercab platforms, leaving millions of older HW3-equipped cars behind.
Beyond the flashy visuals, v15 packs several practical upgrades. A new Bike Rack Mode lets drivers engage FSD even with hitch accessories attached (though it disables reversing, Summon, and Autopark). The system can now automatically ease off the speed near traffic cameras, with an option to override and permanently mute specific cameras after two cancellations. Tesla also promises sharper hazard avoidance for potholes, standing water, and small animals, alongside smoother speed handling in school zones and bad weather.
Meanwhile, navigation learns preferred neighborhood routes and favorite parking spots over time, and the cabin camera can now track driver attention through hats and sunglasses while dialing back nagging alerts at higher speeds. Tesla has a pretty poor reputation for ensuring its drivers stay alert while using FSD, so hopefully this upgrade can improve that.
Still, Reality View raises an obvious question: is a hyper-detailed, video-game-style feed on the center touchscreen a helpful window into the car’s “brain,” or a tempting new distraction? For drivers who find the live feed overwhelming, Tesla includes a map-only option and a stripped-back “quiet mode”.
Tesla’s bold “25x” safety claim also lands amid intense federal scrutiny. The National Highway Traffic Safety Administration (NHTSA) is actively investigating roughly 2.9 million FSD-equipped vehicles over reports of Teslas blowing through red lights, crossing railroad tracks improperly, and driving into oncoming lanes. Alongside dozens of cumulative fatalities linked to Tesla’s driver-assistance systems since 2019, the probe is a stark reminder of the gap between Tesla’s autonomous ambitions and a system that still legally requires a human to keep their eyes on the road.

Jensen Huang is back as Nvidia’s CEO
Unknown
Unknown Publisher
2026-11-23
14:07:53 UTC
23:07:53 KST

Nvidia has reinstated Jensen Huang as its CEO. The company announced on Monday morning that the board “voted unanimously to reinstate Mr. Huang following a series of discussions and meetings with Mr. Huang, after which the Board was satisfied with respect to Mr. Huang’s candor and ability to lead NVIDIA.”
Jensen Huang is back as Nvidia’s CEO
The board voted unanimously to bring back Huang as CEO, President, and Board director.
In its statement, the board says that it received input “from senior levels of the United States government” and determined that “Huang’s reinstatement poses no national security risk.” Donald Trump responded to the board’s decision in a post on Truth Social, saying Nvidia “made the correct and proper decision to return Jensen Huang to the position of CEO… The United States Government has no reason to believe that Jensen’s return to NVIDIA poses any risk to National Security. The Attorney General of the United States has confirmed that the U.S. Government does not possess evidence that he has engaged in any unlawful conduct.”
The board’s statement says that its investigation into reports that Huang was the target of a Chinese spy plot will continue, with Huang’s support and cooperation, while interim CEO Colette Kress will continue as CFO, and Harvey Jones will remain as the recently appointed board chair.
“I am honored to return to NVIDIA and lead one of the most consequential technology companies in the world at the beginning of a new industrial revolution,” Huang said in a statement. “I take this responsibility with the greatest humility and renewed commitment. I want to thank the NVIDIA Board for its confidence and deliberation through this process.” This move comes after Jones said during an earnings call last week that the Nvidia board “does not intend” to return Huang as CEO.
Huang resigned as CEO earlier this month following reports of extramarital affairs with an alleged Chinese spy and two Nvidia employees, including an executive who reported to him. In the wake of his departure, more than 200,000 people, including Tesla CEO Elon Musk, Epic Games CEO Tim Sweeney, Y Combinator CEO Garry Tan, and Uber co-founder Travis Kalanick, signed a letter to Nvidia’s board, calling for his reinstatement.
Nvidia shares tumbled in Huang’s absence, while industry analysts raised concerns about an outsider replacing him at a time when Nvidia is so critical to the AI boom. Nonetheless, the board maintained as recently as last Tuesday that Huang wasn’t coming back.

Jensen Huang Reinstated as Nvidia CEO
Unknown
Unknown Publisher
2026-11-23
14:01:23 UTC
23:01:23 KST

NVIDIA (NASDAQ: NVDA) today announced that effective immediately, Jensen Huang has been reinstated as President and Chief Executive Officer and as a member of the Board. The Board of Directors voted unanimously to reinstate Mr. Huang following a series of discussions and meetings with Mr. Huang, after which the Board was satisfied with respect to Mr. Huang’s candor and ability to lead NVIDIA. In the process of reaching its decision, the Board received input from senior levels of the United States government, and based on that input, the Board is confident that Mr. Huang’s reinstatement poses no national security risk.
“The Board believes Jensen is the right person to lead NVIDIA, and that his reinstatement is in the best interests of our stockholders, employees, partners and customers,” said Harvey C. Jones, Chair of the Board. “We have every confidence in Jensen and look forward to working with him as he resumes his leadership of the Company. We are grateful to Colette Kress for her outstanding service as interim CEO, in addition to her continuing responsibilities as NVIDIA’s Chief Financial Officer.” Harvey Jones will remain as Board chair.
“I am honored to return to NVIDIA and lead one of the most consequential technology companies in the world at the beginning of a new industrial revolution,” said Jensen Huang. “I take this responsibility with the greatest humility and renewed commitment. I want to thank the NVIDIA Board for its confidence and deliberation through this process. I am especially grateful to Harvey Jones for his leadership as Chair, and to Colette Kress and our executive staff for leading NVIDIA during this period. I am also deeply thankful to our employees, shareholders, leaders across the industry and partners around the world for their extraordinary support. Above all, I am grateful for the love and support of my family. I can’t wait to get back to work.”
As previously announced, in light of recent reporting that Mr. Huang has been the target of a foreign intelligence effort, the Board formed a committee comprised solely of independent directors to investigate, and the committee retained independent counsel to assist it in that investigation. The Board reiterated that the committee’s investigation will proceed to conclusion. Mr. Huang fully supports the investigation and is committed to cooperating with it.
The Board also recently formed a National Security and Export Control Committee comprised solely of independent directors to oversee trade compliance and national security risks. The committee is engaging counsel with experience in national security matters, and such counsel will be retaining advisors with expertise in national security matters. NVIDIA is commencing a search to fill a new position, Chief Compliance Officer, to oversee compliance across NVIDIA and report to the Nominating and Corporate Governance Committee. NVIDIA will continue to cooperate with ongoing government investigations and inquiries.

Nvidia Reinstates Jensen Huang as CEO
Unknown
Unknown Publisher
2026-11-23
14:00:18 UTC
23:00:18 KST

Nvidia on Monday announced Jensen Huang has been reinstated as CEO, effective immediately, a dramatic twist in a scandal that hammered the chipmaker's stock price and rocked the tech and artificial intelligence industries.
The board of directors said it unanimously voted to reinstate Huang following "a series of discussions and meetings" with him. The board added that it "received input from senior levels of the United States government" and that it is confident Huang's reinstatement "poses no national security risk."
President Donald Trump told CNBC on Monday that he had been speaking to Nvidia's board "directly and indirectly," and that Huang "did nothing wrong." Trump said he talked to Huang on Sunday and asked him to promise to "work hard."
Huang resigned from his role as chief executive earlier this month after a series of shocking reports about multiple extramarital affairs, including one with an alleged Chinese spy. The New York Times reported on Sunday that the woman, Alice Huang, was in contact with an officer of China's Ministry of State Security during at least part of the time that she was engaging in a relationship with Huang.
Shares of Nvidia closed up 31% following Huang's reinstatement. The stock had shed nearly 33% from the time Huang admitted to an extramarital affair on Nov. 8. The stock closed at $186.64 on Nov.6.
"I am honored to return to NVIDIA and lead one of the most consequential technology companies in the world at the beginning of a new industrial revolution," Huang said in a statement. "I take this responsibility with the greatest humility and renewed commitment. I want to thank the NVIDIA Board for its confidence and deliberation through this process."
Senior government officials briefed Nvidia's board late last week and communicated that no national security laws were violated, according to sources familiar with the discussion who asked to remain anonymous because they were not authorized to speak publicly. That update prompted the board to reach out to Huang, the people said.
Huang and the board held their first conversations since his ouster on Saturday, and the group determined he would return as CEO during a second meeting on Sunday, the people said. Nvidia's cratering stock price played a role in accelerating those conversations.
Trump celebrated Huang's return to Nvidia on Monday, writing in a post on his social media site that the board made "the correct and proper decision" to reinstate him. Trump said the U.S. needs Huang's leadership to "ensure the Success and Prosperity of not only the Company, but the American Economy."
He reiterated that the U.S. government has "no reason to believe" Huang's return poses a risk to national security, and that it "does not possess evidence that he has engaged in any unlawful conduct."
"On a personal note, I have gotten to know Jensen, and he is a Great Patriot and National Treasure," Trump wrote.
Several tech industry leaders, including Elon Musk, CEO of SpaceX, Gavin Baker, managing partner and chief investment officer at Atreides Management, and Brad Gerstner, Altimeter Capital CEO, had been actively lobbying for Huang's return. Musk wrote in a post on X on Monday that the board's "mistake has been corrected."
Nvidia did not immediately respond to CNBC's request for comment.
For weeks following Huang's ouster, the company's board maintained that it was not considering Huang's reinstatement. It appointed Colette Kress, Nvidia's chief financial officer, as the chipmaker's interim CEO, who reiterated that position in an interview with CNBC following Huang's exit. Kress will continue to serve as Nvidia's CFO, according to a filing with the Securities and Exchange Commission.
Nvidia's board formed an independent committee and hired the law firm Wachtell, Lipton, Rosen & Katz to conduct an investigation into Huang and whether company secrets or national security were compromised. The board said Monday that "the committee's investigation will proceed to conclusion" and that Huang is "committed to cooperating with it."
"The Board believes Jensen is the right person to lead NVIDIA, and that his reinstatement is in the best interests of our stockholders, employees, partners and customers," Nvidia's board chair, Harvey Jones, said in a statement. "We have every confidence in Jensen and look forward to working with him as he resumes his leadership of the Company."
Even with Huang back at the helm, Nvidia is facing increased scrutiny over its chip sales in Southeast Asia and China.
Lawmakers have been preparing for investigations into how Huang's affair with the alleged spy may have damaged U.S. AI dominance, influenced his positions on export controls, and impacted his close relationships with Trump and his Cabinet. Huang has been one of the loudest voices opposing strict export controls on advanced chips.
"The reckless decision to bring Jensen Huang back as CEO does not change the fact that Mr. Huang, NVIDIA executives, and the company's board still owe Congress answers about the company's compliance with U.S. export controls," Sen. Elizabeth Warren, D-Mass., said in a statement Monday.
Huang is due for a closed-door transcribed interview with the House Foreign Affairs panel and two other committees on Dec. 1. Nvidia's general counsel, Tim Teter, is scheduled to appear before the panels on Dec. 2. Those dates were set before Huang's return, and John Fisher, who represents Huang, did not comment on whether there are any changes in his plans to testify.
Senate Banking Committee Chairman Tim Scott, R-S.C., said Monday that Huang will publicly testify on Dec. 3 as the sole witness during a hearing titled, "Protecting America's AI Advantage: Oversight of U.S. Export Controls."
"Americans deserve answers about what NVIDIA and the Trump administration knew, when they knew it, and whether sufficient steps were taken to prevent sensitive technology from falling into the wrong hands," Sen. Andy Kim, D-N.J., a member of the Banking Committee, wrote in a post on X on Monday.

Nvidia Reinstates Jensen Huang as CEO
Unknown
Unknown Publisher
2026-11-23
14:00:18 UTC
23:00:18 KST

Nvidia on Monday announced Jensen Huang has been reinstated as CEO, effective immediately, a dramatic twist in a scandal that hammered the chipmaker's stock price and rocked the tech and artificial intelligence industries.
The board of directors said it unanimously voted to reinstate Huang following "a series of discussions and meetings" with him. The board added that it "received input from senior levels of the United States government" and that it is confident Huang's reinstatement "poses no national security risk."
President Donald Trump told CNBC on Monday that he had been speaking to Nvidia's board "directly and indirectly," and that Huang "did nothing wrong." Trump said he talked to Huang on Sunday and asked him to promise to "work hard."
Huang resigned from his role as chief executive earlier this month after a series of shocking reports about multiple extramarital affairs, including one with an alleged Chinese spy. The New York Times reported on Sunday that the woman, Alice Huang, was in contact with an officer of China's Ministry of State Security during at least part of the time that she was engaging in a relationship with Huang.
Shares of Nvidia closed up 31% following Huang's reinstatement. The stock had shed nearly 33% from the time Huang admitted to an extramarital affair on Nov. 8. The stock closed at $186.64 on Nov.6.
"I am honored to return to NVIDIA and lead one of the most consequential technology companies in the world at the beginning of a new industrial revolution," Huang said in a statement. "I take this responsibility with the greatest humility and renewed commitment. I want to thank the NVIDIA Board for its confidence and deliberation through this process."
Senior government officials briefed Nvidia's board late last week and communicated that no national security laws were violated, according to sources familiar with the discussion who asked to remain anonymous because they were not authorized to speak publicly. That update prompted the board to reach out to Huang, the people said.
Huang and the board held their first conversations since his ouster on Saturday, and the group determined he would return as CEO during a second meeting on Sunday, the people said. Nvidia's cratering stock price played a role in accelerating those conversations.
Trump celebrated Huang's return to Nvidia on Monday, writing in a post on his social media site that the board made "the correct and proper decision" to reinstate him. Trump said the U.S. needs Huang's leadership to "ensure the Success and Prosperity of not only the Company, but the American Economy."
He reiterated that the U.S. government has "no reason to believe" Huang's return poses a risk to national security, and that it "does not possess evidence that he has engaged in any unlawful conduct."
"On a personal note, I have gotten to know Jensen, and he is a Great Patriot and National Treasure," Trump wrote.
Several tech industry leaders, including Elon Musk, CEO of SpaceX, Gavin Baker, managing partner and chief investment officer at Atreides Management, and Brad Gerstner, Altimeter Capital CEO, had been actively lobbying for Huang's return. Musk wrote in a post on X on Monday that the board's "mistake has been corrected."
Nvidia did not immediately respond to CNBC's request for comment.
For weeks following Huang's ouster, the company's board maintained that it was not considering Huang's reinstatement. It appointed Colette Kress, Nvidia's chief financial officer, as the chipmaker's interim CEO, who reiterated that position in an interview with CNBC following Huang's exit. Kress will continue to serve as Nvidia's CFO, according to a filing with the Securities and Exchange Commission.
Nvidia's board formed an independent committee and hired the law firm Wachtell, Lipton, Rosen & Katz to conduct an investigation into Huang and whether company secrets or national security were compromised. The board said Monday that "the committee's investigation will proceed to conclusion" and that Huang is "committed to cooperating with it."
"The Board believes Jensen is the right person to lead NVIDIA, and that his reinstatement is in the best interests of our stockholders, employees, partners and customers," Nvidia's board chair, Harvey Jones, said in a statement. "We have every confidence in Jensen and look forward to working with him as he resumes his leadership of the Company."
Even with Huang back at the helm, Nvidia is facing increased scrutiny over its chip sales in Southeast Asia and China.
Lawmakers have been preparing for investigations into how Huang's affair with the alleged spy may have damaged U.S. AI dominance, influenced his positions on export controls, and impacted his close relationships with Trump and his Cabinet. Huang has been one of the loudest voices opposing strict export controls on advanced chips.
"The reckless decision to bring Jensen Huang back as CEO does not change the fact that Mr. Huang, NVIDIA executives, and the company's board still owe Congress answers about the company's compliance with U.S. export controls," Sen. Elizabeth Warren, D-Mass., said in a statement Monday.
Huang is due for a closed-door transcribed interview with the House Foreign Affairs panel and two other committees on Dec. 1. Nvidia's general counsel, Tim Teter, is scheduled to appear before the panels on Dec. 2. Those dates were set before Huang's return, and John Fisher, who represents Huang, did not comment on whether there are any changes in his plans to testify.
Senate Banking Committee Chairman Tim Scott, R-S.C., said Monday that Huang will publicly testify on Dec. 3 as the sole witness during a hearing titled, "Protecting America's AI Advantage: Oversight of U.S. Export Controls."
"Americans deserve answers about what NVIDIA and the Trump administration knew, when they knew it, and whether sufficient steps were taken to prevent sensitive technology from falling into the wrong hands," Sen. Andy Kim, D-N.J., a member of the Banking Committee, wrote in a post on X on Monday.

Uber now lets you book rides that record the drive by default
Unknown
Unknown Publisher
2026-11-23
14:00:00 UTC
23:00:00 KST

Uber is piloting a new feature in the U.S. that offers rides in which drivers have enabled the company’s recording feature, Record My Ride.
The option, which uses the front camera of the Uber driver’s phone to record the entire drive, will show up as “Recorded Rides” in the app.
The ride-hailing company is starting the pilot in Baltimore, Raleigh, Indianapolis, and St. Louis and will expand to more locations later.
Uber said it is testing recorded rides specifically for rides booked at late night or those involving teens. Earlier this year, it introduced a live video streaming feature for rides booked for teenagers. In another test, the company had enabled audio recording for late-night trips in some cities.
The company is also testing a feature that will turn on recording when the app detects the ride going off-course, or when the vehicle might have had an accident.
The company said drivers can opt out of recording through the Settings panel. Similarly, riders can also decline permission to record their rides via an in-app notification.

NariQ: BF16-level Qwen3.8-27B in a 10 GB file
Unknown
Unknown Publisher
2026-11-23
13:42:38 UTC
22:42:38 KST

NariQ: Qwen3.8-27B in a 10 GB file, BF16-level on long-horizon agentic tasks
(Nov 20, 2026) We are releasing NariQ v1 GGUFs of Qwen3.8-27B at 3, 2.5 and 2 bits per weight. Run them on our llama.cpp fork (required).
- The 3-bit and 2.5-bit files match BF16 on long-horizon agent tasks, math and image understanding. The 2-bit file scores lower.
- NariQ is a trellis-coded post-training quantization (PTQ), calibrated on real coding-agent traces and stored in a layout that runs on a single GPU.
- More details are in the tech report.
Each cell gives a percentage and a count in parentheses. For DeepSWE, the count is the mean number of fully solved tasks per round. Across the same three rounds, the mean share of each task's new tests that passed was 83.1% (BF16), 82.9% (3-bit), 83.2% (2.5-bit) and 77.2% (2-bit). One BF16 verifier timeout in round 3 counts as zero (338 of 339 trials graded). Each NariQ variant had all 339 trials graded. Terminal-Bench counts solved trials, and the remaining columns count correct answers. The agent runs use the Claude Code CLI (2.1.309, thinking on, effort max) driving the local model, with a 262,144-token context and the model's thinking-mode sampling (temperature 1.0, top-p 0.95, top-k 20).
KL divergence from the BF16 model on held-out coding-agent traces, against file size. Every point was measured by us with the same code. Lower is better.
DeepSWE fully solved tasks out of 113, against file size. Our NariQ points and solid BF16 line average three rounds with Claude Code. Our GSQ-RCO points use one round. The hollow diamonds show approximate averages from Unsloth's published Dynamic V3.1 chart (mini-swe-agent). Compare each point with the BF16 line for its harness. Higher is better.
Terminal-Bench 2.1 score over 82 tasks × 3 attempts, against file size. Filled circles are our runs (Claude Code); hollow diamonds are Unsloth's reported results (mini-swe-agent), read from its published Dynamic V3.1 chart (hence ≈; its scores are averages over several passes). The two harnesses differ, so compare each point with the BF16 line of its own harness (solid: ours, dashed: Unsloth's). Higher is better.
Quickstart
One command on Linux with an NVIDIA GPU and driver 570 or newer (downloads the fork's binaries and the files, checks them, and fits the context to your GPU):
curl -fsSLO https://github.com/nari-labs/nari-llama.cpp/releases/download/b12431-nariv0/run.sh
bash run.sh 3.0   # or 2.5, 2.0
Or download and serve yourself, with llama-server from the fork:
hf download nari-labs/Qwen3.8-27B-NariQ-GGUF \
    Qwen3.8-27B-NariQ-3.0bpw.gguf Qwen3.8-27B-NariQ-mmproj-BF16.gguf --local-dir .
llama-server -m Qwen3.8-27B-NariQ-3.0bpw.gguf --mmproj Qwen3.8-27B-NariQ-mmproj-BF16.gguf \
    -ngl 99 -c 131072 --jinja --port 8080 \
    --temp 1.0 --top-p 0.95 --top-k 20 --min-p 0
The sampling flags are the thinking-mode settings our scores used. -c 131072 fits a 24 GB GPU with little to spare;
lower it if a display shares the GPU, or let run.sh pick the largest context that fits. Use -c 262144 (the model's
full context) on 32 GB or more. GPU memory with the vision projector:
Prebuilt binaries, the Docker image, supported GPUs and build steps: the fork's README.
Decode speed on one NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 (Blackwell), single session, 512 greedy output tokens, tokens/s:
NariQ on our llama.cpp fork with -fa on -ctk bf16 -ctv bf16 -t 1 -tb 16 --backend-sampling --samplers temperature (greedy sampling on the GPU, BF16 KV cache). UD-IQ3_XXS is Unsloth's file on llama.cpp, measured earlier on the same GPU type.
Known limits and future work
- Requires a NariQ-aware runtime: for now, our llama.cpp fork. Stock llama.cpp, Ollama and LM Studio can't load the new tensor types yet; we hope to merge support into upstream llama.cpp. Our internal SGLang plugin and our inference engine built on Resin are a work in progress for potential public release.
- The llama.cpp fork needs more work across hardware. The current fork is focused on SM120 performance (e.g. RTX PRO 6000). Ampere, Ada and Hopper GPUs and Apple M-series GPUs are slower than comparable GGUFs for now; we would love open-source contributions for speedups, as this has been a solo effort. GPUs older than Ampere fall back to slow CPU execution, and AMD and Intel GPUs are not supported yet. GeForce cards have not been tested yet; tested GPUs are RTX PRO 6000, H100, L4 and A10.
- Apple M-series (Metal): build from source; on an M3 Pro (18 GB) decode is about 3.8 tok/s with the 3.0bpw file (4.9 with 2.0bpw), far slower than CUDA. Other Macs are untested.
- The 2-bit file is weaker overall; use the 2.5-bit or 3-bit for higher overall performance.
- The vision tower stays in BF16; only the language model is quantized. The MTP (multi-token prediction) module is not included yet; it is still to be quantized.
- KV-cache and activation quantization are next, so the files fit long contexts on 16 GB consumer GPUs and run faster. Today the KV cache and activations stay in BF16.
Contact us
For technical consulting, compute sponsorships, or partnership inquiries, please contact founders@narilabs.com.
License
These files inherit the base model's Apache-2.0 license; see NOTICE.
Acknowledgements
Thanks to the Qwen team for the amazing Qwen3.8-27B, and to the Unsloth, ExLlamaV3, GSQ-RCO (IST Austria DASLab) and GGML teams, whose open-source work this release builds on. NariQ's trellis codes build on QTIP (Tseng et al., 2024).
Citation
If you use NariQ, please cite the technical report:
@techreport{kim2026nariq,
  title       = {NariQ: Sub-3-Bit Quantization for Long-Horizon Agentic Tasks},
  author      = {Doyeob (Toby) Kim},
  institution = {Nari Labs},
  year        = {2026},
  month       = nov,

  archivePrefix = {arXiv},
  url         = {https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26157}
}
Qwen3.8-27B
Following the widespread community adoption of the Qwen3.5 and Qwen3.6 series, we are pleased to introduce Qwen3.8, the most capable generation in the Qwen open-model family to date.
Built on the architectural foundation of Qwen3.5, Qwen3.8 delivers substantial gains across coding, professional work, research, and long-horizon agentic tasks. Qwen3.8-27B brings these advances to a compact, deployment-friendly dense model: a native vision-language model that understands images and videos, with flexible thinking control, designed to carry complex, multi-step tasks through to completion with greater reliability.
Qwen3.8 Highlights
Qwen3.8-27B features the following enhancements:
- Core Capabilities: Comprehensive improvements across coding, professional work, research, and long-horizon agentic tasks.
- Agent Execution: Stronger autonomous planning and better handling of environment feedback, leading to more reliable end-to-end task completion.
- Downstream Compatibility: Broader support for popular harnesses and development tools, making it easier to integrate into your existing stack.
- Flexible Thinking Control: Thinking mode is on by default and can be disabled per request; reasoning depth can be tuned with reasoning_effort, and reasoning context from historical messages is retained via preserve_thinking.
- Vision-Language Understanding: Native support for image and video understanding, from STEM diagrams and documents to hour-scale videos.
Model Overview
- Type: Causal Language Model with Vision Encoder
- Training Stage: Pre-training & Post-training
- Language Model
  - Number of Parameters: 27B
  - Hidden Dimension: 5120
  - Token Embedding: 248,320 (Padded)
  - Number of Layers: 64
  - Hidden Layout: 16 × (3 × (Gated DeltaNet → FFN) → 1 × (Gated Attention → FFN))
  - Gated DeltaNet:
    - Number of Linear Attention Heads: 48 for V and 16 for QK
    - Head Dimension: 128
  - Gated Attention:
    - Number of Attention Heads: 24 for Q and 4 for KV
    - Head Dimension: 256
    - Rotary Position Embedding Dimension: 64
  - Feed Forward Network:
    - Intermediate Dimension: 17,408
  - LM Output: 248,320 (Padded)
  - MTP (Multi-Token Prediction): trained with multiple steps
- Context Length: 262,144 natively and extensible up to 1,000,000 tokens.
Best Practices
To achieve optimal performance, we recommend the following settings:
- Sampling Parameters: We suggest using the following sets of sampling parameters: 
  - Thinking Mode: temperature=1.0, top_p=0.95, top_k=20, min_p=0.0, presence_penalty=0.0, repetition_penalty=1.0
  - Instruct (or non-thinking) mode: temperature=0.7, top_p=0.80, top_k=20, min_p=0.0, presence_penalty=1.5, repetition_penalty=1.0
 For supported frameworks, you can adjust the presence_penalty parameter between 0 and 2 to reduce endless repetition. However, using a higher value may occasionally result in language mixing and a slight decrease in model performance.
- Adequate Output Length: To optimize performance on agentic tasks, we recommend allocating sufficient output length to allow the model to generate detailed and comprehensive responses. For frameworks that support separate token limits for internal reasoning and final outputs, we suggest the following configuration within the 1M context length: 
  - Reasoning Content: Set the maximum output length to 262,144 tokens.
  - Final Response: Set the maximum output length to 131,072 tokens.
These settings provide the necessary capacity for complex reasoning while ensuring ample space for high-quality final deliverables.
- Processing Ultra-Long Texts: Qwen3.8-27B natively supports context lengths of up to 262,144 tokens. For long-horizon tasks where the total length (including both input and output) exceeds this limit, we recommend using RoPE scaling techniques to handle long texts effectively, e.g., YaRN.
- Long Video Understanding: To optimize inference efficiency for plain text and images, the size parameter in the released video_preprocessor_config.json is conservatively configured. It is recommended to set the longest_edge parameter in the video_preprocessor_config file to 469,762,048 (corresponding to 224k video tokens) to enable higher frame-rate sampling for hour-scale videos and thereby achieve superior performance. For example,
{"longest_edge": 469762048, "shortest_edge": 4096}
Alternatively, override the default values via engine startup parameters. For implementation details, refer to: vLLM / SGLang.
Citation
@misc{qwen38,
    title = {{Qwen3.8-Max}: A New Bar for Coding and Cowork},
    url = {https://qwen.ai/blog?id=qwen3.8},
    author = {{Qwen Team}},
    month = {August},
    year = {2026}
}

Best Practices

- Sampling Parameters: We suggest using the following sets of sampling parameters: 
  - Thinking Mode: temperature=1.0, top_p=0.95, top_k=20, min_p=0.0, presence_penalty=0.0, repetition_penalty=1.0
  - Instruct (or non-thinking) mode: temperature=0.7, top_p=0.80, top_k=20, min_p=0.0, presence_penalty=1.5, repetition_penalty=1.0

- Adequate Output Length: To optimize performance on agentic tasks, we recommend allocating sufficient output length to allow the model to generate detailed and comprehensive responses. For frameworks that support separate token limits for internal reasoning and final outputs, we suggest the following configuration within the 1M context length: 
  - Reasoning Content: Set the maximum output length to 262,144 tokens.
  - Final Response: Set the maximum output length to 131,072 tokens.
These settings provide the necessary capacity for complex reasoning while ensuring ample space for high-quality final deliverables.
- Processing Ultra-Long Texts: Qwen3.8-27B natively supports context lengths of up to 262,144 tokens. For long-horizon tasks where the total length (including both input and output) exceeds this limit, we recommend using RoPE scaling techniques to handle long texts effectively, e.g., YaRN.
- Long Video Understanding: To optimize inference efficiency for plain text and images, the size parameter in the released video_preprocessor_config.json is conservatively configured. It is recommended to set the longest_edge parameter in the video_preprocessor_config file to 469,762,048 (corresponding to 224k video tokens) to enable higher frame-rate sampling for hour-scale videos and thereby achieve superior performance. For example,
{"longest_edge": 469762048, "shortest_edge": 4096}
Alternatively, override the default values via engine startup parameters. For implementation details, refer to: vLLM / SGLang.
Citation
@misc{qwen38,
    title = {{Qwen3.8-Max}: A New Bar for Coding and Cowork},
    url = {https://qwen.ai/blog?id=qwen3.8},
    author = {{Qwen Team}},
    month = {August},
    year = {2026}
}
- Downloads last month
  8,795
We're not able to determine the quantization variants.

Amazon is taking orders for the Moto Tag 3 before it’s even been announced
Unknown
Unknown Publisher
2026-11-23
13:09:32 UTC
22:09:32 KST

Motorola’s unannounced Moto Tag 3 has been listed for sale online, just five months after the Moto Tag 2 went on sale in the US, and before any official announcement of a follow-up. The listing on Amazon UK was spotted by X user @matteventu, and shows the tracker in a “Pantone Lucent White” color option, with a price of £24.96 (about $33) and a 1-4 month shipping estimate.
Amazon is taking orders for the Moto Tag 3 before it’s even been announced
The Amazon UK listing for Motorola’s new tracking tag has been live since Friday.
A second image shared by leaker Evan Blass also shows a vibrant purple version of the new tracking tag, which is described as “Pantone Indigo Blue” on a separate Amazon Italy listing. Both are new color options, with the Moto Tag 2 only currently available in orange or a grey-ish beige. While the Moto Tag 3 name suggests this is a follow-up to the Moto Tag 2, there don’t appear to be any major hardware differences between the two trackers.
Both use a replaceable CR2032 battery, claim to provide more than 500 days of battery life, and support UWB in Android Find Hub, among other similarities. The XT2561-1 model number seen on the back of the Moto Tag 3 images also matches that of the Moto Tag 2. That could mean that the Moto Tag 3 name is an error and these are just new color options for the Tag 2.
We’ve asked Motorola for clarification and will update if we hear back.

Show HN: News Blocklist – a DNS blocklist of 14k news and current-affairs sites
Unknown
Unknown Publisher
2026-11-23
13:02:52 UTC
22:02:52 KST

A DNS blocklist of news and current-affairs websites, for people who want a break from the news.
It covers newspapers, broadcasters' news sites, news agencies, news magazines, political news and commentary, business news, local news and news aggregators, worldwide. Major and regional outlets are curated in this repository. The long tail of smaller news sites is pulled from Wikidata, filtered automatically, checked against DNS, and refreshed every week.
Works with Pi-hole, AdGuard Home, uBlock Origin, AdGuard, dnsmasq, hosts files and any blocker that subscribes to a list by URL.
Both block the same major sites. news adds thousands of smaller ones.
Each list comes in five formats. Use the URL as-is to subscribe; lists update when the data changes and at least weekly.
- adblock, wildcard and dnsmasq entries block a domain and all its subdomains. Use one of these if your blocker supports it.
- hosts and domains list exact hostnames, because a hosts file can't match
subdomains. Each site is expanded to the names people actually visit (www., m., amp., edition. and so on) using DNS checks.
Pi-hole · Lists (v6) or Adlists (v5) → add
https://raw.githubusercontent.com/ruairidhflint/news-blocklist/main/lists/adblock/news.txt
→ run Update Gravity. Pi-hole v6 (and v5 with FTL 5.22 or later) reads the adblock
format and blocks subdomains; on older versions use lists/domains/news.txt.
AdGuard Home · Filters → DNS blocklists → Add blocklist → Add a custom list →
paste the adblock/news.txt URL.
uBlock Origin · Dashboard → Filter lists → Import (at the bottom) → paste the
adblock/news.txt URL → Apply changes. Blocked pages show uBlock's warning page.
AdGuard (browser extension and apps) · Filters → Custom → Add custom filter →
paste the adblock/news.txt URL.
dnsmasq / OpenWrt · download lists/dnsmasq/news.txt into /etc/dnsmasq.d/ (or add
it as a conf-file) and restart dnsmasq.
Hosts file (macOS, Linux, Windows) · append lists/hosts/news-lite.txt (or news.txt)
to /etc/hosts (C:\Windows\System32\drivers\etc\hosts on Windows). Turn off your
browser's "secure DNS" / DNS-over-HTTPS, or it will skip the hosts file.
Blocked: general news (national, regional and local), politics, world affairs, business and economic news, opinion and commentary on current affairs, news agencies, broadcasters' news sites and 24-hour news channels, news and current-affairs magazines, tabloids, fact-checkers, news aggregators and the news sections of web portals.
Not blocked: sport-only, entertainment and celebrity, technology, gaming and science publications, lifestyle magazines, trade press, academic journals, government sites, and TV or radio streaming services (unless they're the same site as the news).
The curated lists follow this line. The part pulled from Wikidata is filtered to follow it, but the filters rely on Wikidata's own descriptions and miss some sites (see Limitations). The line isn't always sharp either. If you think a site is on the wrong side of it, open an issue.
DNS blocking works on whole hostnames. It can't block example.com/news and leave
example.com/weather working. When news is the main thing a host serves, it's blocked
anyway, and anything else on that host goes with it. Where a company runs other services
on separate hostnames (email, logins, streaming apps), only the news hostnames are listed.
These are the hosts where blocking news also blocks something else:
To keep one of these working, add an exception for it in your blocker.
- News inside other apps and sites isn't blocked: social media feeds, search results ("Top stories"), YouTube, podcasts, and phone news widgets that use shared platform APIs. Pair this list with a social-media list if those are a problem.
- Browsers with DNS-over-HTTPS bypass hosts files and local DNS blockers. Turn it off or point it at your blocker.
- The Wikidata part is filtered, not reviewed. It adds about 10,000 smaller sites automatically. Outlets that Wikidata classes as gaming, sports or trade publications are dropped, and anything whose description, genre or hostname suggests a specialist outlet goes to a review queue instead of the list. Many Wikidata entries have no description, so some specialist, defunct or off-topic sites still get through. Popular domains are never added automatically. If something's wrong, report it.
- The curated core was compiled with an AI model, not by a person working site by site. See How it's built. Expect some mistakes at this scale.
data/curated/*.txt     curated outlets, by region                 ─┐
data/generated/        news outlets from Wikidata, DNS results    ─┼─► scripts/build.py ─► lists/
data/allow.txt         domains never blocked                      ─┘
- Curated outlets (data/curated/) are the core: major and regional news sites in
every region, plus aggregators. The first version was compiled with an AI model (Claude).
It went through the most popular domains that Wikidata lists as news outlets one by one,
then added outlets Wikidata was missing from its own knowledge. Every hostname resolves in
DNS (checked weekly), and most additions were checked against the site's live homepage.
Entries are hostnames; a comment starting mixed: documents what else blocking that host
breaks.
- Wikidata supplies the long tail. scripts/refresh.py asks for everything that is a
newspaper, news website, news agency, news magazine or news channel (the classes are in data/wikidata-classes.txt) and still has an official website. It then drops:
  - defunct outlets and old website addresses;
  - outlets Wikidata also classes as something out of scope: video game, sports and trade publications, academic journals, shops, databases and the like;
  - outlets whose Wikidata genre or main subject is sport, music, film, technology, science, fashion or similar;
  - government, military and education domains;
  - websites that are just a page on a shared host;
  - domains in data/allow.txt.
Sites whose Wikidata description, genre or hostname only suggests a specialist outlet
(gaming, tech, trade press, weather, crypto …) aren't added either. They go to
data/generated/review.txt, because the same words turn up in real newspapers too (the
Weatherford Democrat, the Daily Gamecock).
- A popularity guard. A site on a domain in the Tranco
top 25,000 is never added automatically, so a bad Wikidata edit can't block a major
site. Those go to the review queue too. Each queued site waits for a person to add it to
a curated file or to allow.txt.
- DNS checks remove dead sites and find the subdomains each site uses, for the hosts-format lists.
- Tests check every build. A reference set of major news sites must be blocked in every list and format, and sites that aren't news (Google, the NHS, Sky Sports, Yahoo Mail and others) must not be. A build that fails isn't published.
- GitHub Actions rebuilds the lists on every change and refreshes from Wikidata every Monday. A refresh that would shrink the list sharply is refused, so a Wikidata outage can't empty it.
Run it yourself with Python 3.10+ and no dependencies:
python3 scripts/refresh.py   # needs network: Wikidata, DNS, Tranco, Public Suffix List
python3 scripts/build.py     # offline: writes lists/ and the stats above
python3 scripts/test_lists.py
Missing a site, or a site blocked by mistake? Open an issue or
see CONTRIBUTING.md: most changes are one line in data/curated/ or
data/allow.txt.
The list and scripts are MIT licensed.
- Outlet data from Wikidata, available under CC0.
- Site popularity from the Tranco list (Le Pochat et al., NDSS 2019), used only to decide what needs review.
- Domain parsing with the Public Suffix List (MPL 2.0), downloaded at build time.

Anthropic's Safety-Conscious Corporate Culture Still Envisions Superintelligent AI
Unknown
Unknown Publisher
2026-11-23
12:34:00 UTC
21:34:00 KST

Anthropic's Safety-Conscious Corporate Culture Still Envisions Superintelligent AI (msn.com) 60
			
		 	
				The Washington Post looks at Anthropic's "unique" corporate culture, where CEO Dario Amodei "leads his workforce through essays, long memos posted in Slack and twice-monthly all-hands talks jokingly known as 'Dario Vision Quests'...
"The CEO's output has established a constellation of beliefs treated as obvious, urgent and righteous inside the company: AI that supersedes humans is inevitable, the United States must limit China's AI development, and Claude might be conscious."
Inside this tight-knit culture, Anthropic staffers, who call themselves Ants, get to feel that they are working on the world's most urgent problems, with the smartest people, in a shared mission to safeguard humanity, according to interviews with more than a dozen people familiar with the company's ethos, including several current and former employees. At its core is a belief that Anthropic — and Amodei — know what's best for the world...   "A pervasive thing at Anthropic is: We're going to put on our capes and we're going to be the responsible ones, and we're going to accrue great power, but ... because we're doing it responsibly, it's responsible to acquire more power," said a former employee who left the company this year and, like others, spoke on the condition of anonymity to avoid retaliation from the company....
Its message to investors fit with a vision of the future that Amodei often sketches out for his staff, people familiar with the company said, in which AI companies have the power of nation-states.  After nearly six years of telling his workers that powerful AI will force society's leaders to makep difficult decisions, Amodei is now one of the powers who must decide how to act, in a major test of the corporate culture he has created....   [Amodei] set out to create a more safety-conscious culture when he co-founded Anthropic in 2021 with six people from his inner circle, most of them from OpenAI.   In an internal memo the following year, Amodei laid out the stakes. "The current trajectory of AI development makes me think that there's a 20 to 30 percent chance that AGI will lead to the destruction or permanent stunting of humanity," he wrote, referring to the concept of artificial general intelligence, previously unreported court records show. He added that truly capable AI "seems more powerful/dangerous than nuclear weapons." 
Amodei was careful about those he hired to work on that existential challenge. Recruits were screened with a "culture interview," even for back office and policy roles, according to former employees and an archived copy of the company's careers page... It's "a bit of an 'are you ready for the cult' interview," an Anthropic hiring manager texted the unsuccessful candidate, according to a screenshot reviewed by The Post...
Before ringing in Anthropic's record initial public offering at Times Square in New York on Thursday, Amodei delivered a short speech with some of his greatest hits.  "To state it unequivocally, we will pace the training and deployment of AI systems, taking as long as is necessary to have confidence that they are safe and will remain under human control," Amodei said.

Win4wine – Run Windows Shell as the Linux Desktop on Wine
Unknown
Unknown Publisher
2026-11-23
12:28:11 UTC
21:28:11 KST

Linux のデスクトップを、Windows XP のシェルで。
日本語 · English
Important
個人の実験的なプロジェクトです。 日常の作業環境として使えるほど完成してはいません。 動作を確かめているのは、新しくインストールした Debian 13 に導入スクリプトで入れた場合だけです。 すでに使っている環境（ほかのデスクトップ環境やディスプレイマネージャが入っているものなど）への導入は試していません。 試すときは、仮想マシンや試験用のマシンをおすすめします。
Windows XP の explorer.exe を、パッチを当てた Wine の上で Linux のデスクトップシェルとして動かすプロジェクトです。
タスクバー、スタートメニュー、デスクトップ、フォルダウィンドウは XP 本物のものです。Linux のアプリも、XP の枠とタスクバーで扱えます。
Microsoft のファイルは同梱していません。お手元の XP の ISO から、必要なファイルだけを取り出して使います。
XP の ISO が無い方は、XP のシェルの代わりに ReactOS のシェル（Windows 2000 風のクラシックの見た目）でも 試せます。ReactOS は自由なソフトウェアなので、導入スクリプトが公式のリリースから取得します（docs/reactos.md）。
Windows 7 の ISO があれば、Windows 7 のシェル（Windows 7 ベーシックの見た目）でも試せます。まだ実験的な試作です（docs/windows7.md）。
- XP のシェル: Luna（またはクラシック）のタスクバー、スタートメニュー、デスクトップ、マイ コンピュータ、フォルダウィンドウ。
- Linux アプリとの統合: Linux のウィンドウに XP の枠が付き、タスクバー、Alt+Tab、最小化・最大化が XP と同じように動きます。 Linux のアプリは「すべてのプログラム」に並びます。
- 通知領域: Linux のトレイアイコン（StatusNotifierItem と XEmbed）が XP の通知領域に出ます。 XP 自身のネットワーク・バッテリー・音量のアイコンと、そのフライアウト（音量、電源メーター、ワイヤレス ネットワーク接続の状態）も 出ます。NetworkManager と PipeWire / PulseAudio の状態から作ります（AshesTom さんのフォークの XP Explorer から）。 ワイヤレス ネットワークの一覧から、初めてのネットワークにも XP のネットワーク キーの画面で接続できます。メニューや画面の文字は ISO の XP のファイルから読みます。
- XP Explorer からの追加: フォルダの左のタスク ペイン、画面のプロパティ（Windows XP とクラシックのテーマ、スタイルと配色。
配色は ISO の HIVEDEF.INF と themeui.dll から作ります）、画像の縮小版とプレビュー、XP のサウンド（起動、ログオン、ログオフも）、
ネットワーク接続、Internet Explorer 6、Windows Media Player 9、スタートメニューの左の列（インターネット、電子メール、
よく使うプログラム）。どれもお手元の ISO のファイルで動きます（prefix/extras.py）。
- キーボードの配列: 最初は Linux のシステムの配列（localectl）です。コントロール パネルの「地域と言語のオプション」→
「言語」→「詳細」（XP 自身のもの）で入力言語を足したり既定を変えたりすると、セッションの配列（Wayland では labwc、
X11 では setxkbmap）がすぐに従います。2 つ以上なら XP と同じ左 Alt+Shift で切り替わります（session/keyboard.py）。
- 日本語入力: fcitx5 + Mozc です。Ctrl+Space（または全角/半角）で切り替えます。GTK、Qt、Wine のアプリで使えます。
- 日本語版と英語版の XP: どちらの ISO でも動きます。言語は ISO から自動で判断し、字体（英語版は Tahoma など）、 ロケール、ようこそ画面をその XP に合わせます（英語版では日本語入力は設定しません）。
- Windows のアプリ: WoW64 の Wine 11.0 で、32bit と 64bit のアプリが動きます。端末で wine setup.exe と打つと、
インストーラーが作るショートカットは XP のスタートメニューに入ります。
- XP のようこそ画面: LightDM の greeter として、XP のようこそ画面とログオフの画面を出せます（任意）。 シェルが起動するまでは XP と同じ大きな「ようこそ」の画面を出します。
- XP のキー操作: Windows キー、Ctrl+Esc、Win+R、Win+E が使えます。
- クイック起動: タスクバーの右クリック →「ツール バー」→「クイック起動」で出せます（XP と ReactOS。Windows 7 は実機と同じく 「新規ツール バー」でクイック起動のフォルダを選びます）。スタートメニューから Linux のアプリのショートカットも置けます。 「デスクトップの表示」も実機と同じく、もう一度押すと窓が元に戻ります。
- 画面の拡大（DPI）: XP と同じ「DPI 設定」で、文字や部品を大きくできます（96・120・144 など）。
- 落ちても戻れる: XP のシェルが繰り返し落ちると、Wine 標準の explorer と xterm に切り替わります。
- Wayland のセッション（任意・X11 のセッション以上に実験的）: X11 のウィンドウマネージャーの代わりに、パッチを当てた labwc で 動かすセッションです（XP、ReactOS、Windows 7 のシェル）。モニターごとの倍率や 125%・150% などの倍率が使え、シェルはぼけずに描かれます。 Wayland のアプリにもシェルの枠が付きます。作ったばかりで、確かめた範囲は X11 のセッションより狭く、既知の制限もあります（docs/wayland.md）。 ふだん試すなら X11 のセッションをおすすめします。
- ReactOS のシェル（任意）: XP の ISO の代わりに ReactOS 0.4.16 の explorer で動かすセッションです。日本語と英語に対応します。 XP のシェルと並べて入れられます。
- Windows 7 のシェル（任意・実験的）: Windows 7 SP1 x64 の explorer で動かすセッションです（Windows 7 ベーシックの見た目。 Aero のガラスはまだありません）。スタートメニュー、タスクバー、通知領域、フォルダの窓が動き、Linux のアプリもアプリごとに タスクバーにまとまります。確かめたのは日本語版と、その文字を英語の言語パックのものに入れ替えた英語版です。
- Windows XP Professional SP3（x86）の ISO（日本語版か英語版）をご自身で用意してください。
- 新しくインストールした Debian 13 に、ログインして使う利用者として入り、次を実行します。
git clone https://github.com/mikuta0407/win4wine.git ~/win4wine
cd ~/win4wine
scripts/setup.sh --media /path/to/WindowsXPSP3.iso --greeter
- 再起動すると、XP のようこそ画面からそのまま XP のシェルに入ります。
XP の ISO が無いときは、ReactOS のシェルで試せます（ISO は要りません）。
scripts/setup.sh --shell reactos --lang ja   # 英語なら --lang en
ログイン画面でセッション「ReactOS Shell (Wine, X11)」を選びます。
Windows 7 のシェル（実験的）は、Windows 7 SP1 x64 の ISO から作ります。
scripts/setup.sh --shell win7 --media /path/to/Windows7SP1x64.iso
ログイン画面でセッション「Windows 7 Shell (Wine, X11)」を選びます。
詳しい手順とオプションは 🔗 導入手順（INSTALL.md） にあります。Wine をソースからビルドするので、4 コアで 1 時間半ほどかかります。 テーマ（XP のクラシックなど）と文字の大きさは、後から変えられます（見た目と文字の大きさを変える）。
Warning
- Windows XP（と Windows 7）の ISO はご自身で入手してください。 このリポジトリにも導入スクリプトにも、Microsoft のファイルを 取得する処理はありません。ISO と取り出したファイルの扱いは、Microsoft の使用許諾に従ってください。
- 利用は自己責任でお願いします。 データの損失や不具合について、作者は責任を負いません。
- Microsoft とも Wine プロジェクトとも関係のない、非公式のプロジェクトです。
- 確かめているのは日本語版と英語版の XP SP3（x86）だけです。ほかの言語の XP も英語版と同じ設定で入れるので動くかもしれませんが、
一切試していません（setup.sh は警告を出して続けます）。
LightDM ─ XP のようこそ画面 (greeter/)
   └─ XP Shell セッション (session/supervisor.py)
        ├─ Wine 11.0 + 151 本のパッチ (wine/patches/)
        │    └─ XP の explorer.exe, shell32.dll, …（ISO から取り出したもの。ReactOS や Windows 7 のシェルではそれらのもの）
        ├─ bridge-wm: X11 のウィンドウマネージャー + Win32 の代理ウィンドウ (bridge-wm/)
        │    （Wayland のセッションでは、ウィンドウマネージャーの代わりにパッチを当てた labwc。wayland/）
        └─ fcitx5 + Mozc, トレイの仲介 (bridge-wm/tray.py)
仕組みや設計の詳しい説明は docs/ にまとめています。
- loss32: Wine の上の Win32 のソフトウェアだけでデスクトップを作る Linux ディストリビューションの計画です。 ReactOS の部品も使います。win4wine が XP 本物のシェルを Debian の上で動かすのに対し、loss32 は自由なソフトウェアだけで作ります。
- Windows 由来のバイナリには手を加えません。違いは Wine とその周辺で吸収します。
- Microsoft のバイナリを逆アセンブルしません。挙動は公開 API と、実際の XP の上で動かす自作のプローブで調べています。
- Microsoft のファイル、取り出したもの、Wine の prefix は、Git 管理外の localres/ と .local/ にだけ置きます。
- AshesTom さん（フォーク）: XP の画面のプロパティ、
サウンド、キーボードの配列、通知領域の XP のアイコン、スタートメニューの左の列、Internet Explorer 6 と Windows Media Player 9、
起動中のようこそ画面、タイトルバーのアニメーションと小さいアイコン、Arch Linux への対応、xp.sh など。
LGPL-2.1-or-later（Wine と同じ）です。対象はこのリポジトリのコードとパッチだけです。
ただし wayland/patches/labwc/ は labwc と同じ GPL-2.0-only、wayland/patches/wlroots/ は wlroots と同じ MIT です。
Windows XP と Windows 7 のファイルと、スクリーンショットに写っている XP と Windows 7 の画面の著作権は Microsoft にあります。
ReactOS のシェルで使う ReactOS のファイルは、ReactOS のライセンス（GPL-2.0-or-later、DLL の多くは LGPL-2.1-or-later）に従います。

Win4wine – Run Windows Shell as the Linux Desktop on Wine
Unknown
Unknown Publisher
2026-11-23
12:28:11 UTC
21:28:11 KST

Linux のデスクトップを、Windows XP のシェルで。
日本語 · English
Important
個人の実験的なプロジェクトです。 日常の作業環境として使えるほど完成してはいません。 動作を確かめているのは、新しくインストールした Debian 13 に導入スクリプトで入れた場合だけです。 すでに使っている環境（ほかのデスクトップ環境やディスプレイマネージャが入っているものなど）への導入は試していません。 試すときは、仮想マシンや試験用のマシンをおすすめします。
Windows XP の explorer.exe を、パッチを当てた Wine の上で Linux のデスクトップシェルとして動かすプロジェクトです。
タスクバー、スタートメニュー、デスクトップ、フォルダウィンドウは XP 本物のものです。Linux のアプリも、XP の枠とタスクバーで扱えます。
Microsoft のファイルは同梱していません。お手元の XP の ISO から、必要なファイルだけを取り出して使います。
XP の ISO が無い方は、XP のシェルの代わりに ReactOS のシェル（Windows 2000 風のクラシックの見た目）でも 試せます。ReactOS は自由なソフトウェアなので、導入スクリプトが公式のリリースから取得します（docs/reactos.md）。
Windows 7 の ISO があれば、Windows 7 のシェル（Windows 7 ベーシックの見た目）でも試せます。まだ実験的な試作です（docs/windows7.md）。
- XP のシェル: Luna（またはクラシック）のタスクバー、スタートメニュー、デスクトップ、マイ コンピュータ、フォルダウィンドウ。
- Linux アプリとの統合: Linux のウィンドウに XP の枠が付き、タスクバー、Alt+Tab、最小化・最大化が XP と同じように動きます。 Linux のアプリは「すべてのプログラム」に並びます。
- 通知領域: Linux のトレイアイコン（StatusNotifierItem と XEmbed）が XP の通知領域に出ます。 XP 自身のネットワーク・バッテリー・音量のアイコンと、そのフライアウト（音量、電源メーター、ワイヤレス ネットワーク接続の状態）も 出ます。NetworkManager と PipeWire / PulseAudio の状態から作ります（AshesTom さんのフォークの XP Explorer から）。 ワイヤレス ネットワークの一覧から、初めてのネットワークにも XP のネットワーク キーの画面で接続できます。メニューや画面の文字は ISO の XP のファイルから読みます。
- XP Explorer からの追加: フォルダの左のタスク ペイン、画面のプロパティ（Windows XP とクラシックのテーマ、スタイルと配色。
配色は ISO の HIVEDEF.INF と themeui.dll から作ります）、画像の縮小版とプレビュー、XP のサウンド（起動、ログオン、ログオフも）、
ネットワーク接続、Internet Explorer 6、Windows Media Player 9、スタートメニューの左の列（インターネット、電子メール、
よく使うプログラム）。どれもお手元の ISO のファイルで動きます（prefix/extras.py）。
- キーボードの配列: 最初は Linux のシステムの配列（localectl）です。コントロール パネルの「地域と言語のオプション」→
「言語」→「詳細」（XP 自身のもの）で入力言語を足したり既定を変えたりすると、セッションの配列（Wayland では labwc、
X11 では setxkbmap）がすぐに従います。2 つ以上なら XP と同じ左 Alt+Shift で切り替わります（session/keyboard.py）。
- 日本語入力: fcitx5 + Mozc です。Ctrl+Space（または全角/半角）で切り替えます。GTK、Qt、Wine のアプリで使えます。
- 日本語版と英語版の XP: どちらの ISO でも動きます。言語は ISO から自動で判断し、字体（英語版は Tahoma など）、 ロケール、ようこそ画面をその XP に合わせます（英語版では日本語入力は設定しません）。
- Windows のアプリ: WoW64 の Wine 11.0 で、32bit と 64bit のアプリが動きます。端末で wine setup.exe と打つと、
インストーラーが作るショートカットは XP のスタートメニューに入ります。
- XP のようこそ画面: LightDM の greeter として、XP のようこそ画面とログオフの画面を出せます（任意）。 シェルが起動するまでは XP と同じ大きな「ようこそ」の画面を出します。
- XP のキー操作: Windows キー、Ctrl+Esc、Win+R、Win+E が使えます。
- クイック起動: タスクバーの右クリック →「ツール バー」→「クイック起動」で出せます（XP と ReactOS。Windows 7 は実機と同じく 「新規ツール バー」でクイック起動のフォルダを選びます）。スタートメニューから Linux のアプリのショートカットも置けます。 「デスクトップの表示」も実機と同じく、もう一度押すと窓が元に戻ります。
- 画面の拡大（DPI）: XP と同じ「DPI 設定」で、文字や部品を大きくできます（96・120・144 など）。
- 落ちても戻れる: XP のシェルが繰り返し落ちると、Wine 標準の explorer と xterm に切り替わります。
- Wayland のセッション（任意・X11 のセッション以上に実験的）: X11 のウィンドウマネージャーの代わりに、パッチを当てた labwc で 動かすセッションです（XP、ReactOS、Windows 7 のシェル）。モニターごとの倍率や 125%・150% などの倍率が使え、シェルはぼけずに描かれます。 Wayland のアプリにもシェルの枠が付きます。作ったばかりで、確かめた範囲は X11 のセッションより狭く、既知の制限もあります（docs/wayland.md）。 ふだん試すなら X11 のセッションをおすすめします。
- ReactOS のシェル（任意）: XP の ISO の代わりに ReactOS 0.4.16 の explorer で動かすセッションです。日本語と英語に対応します。 XP のシェルと並べて入れられます。
- Windows 7 のシェル（任意・実験的）: Windows 7 SP1 x64 の explorer で動かすセッションです（Windows 7 ベーシックの見た目。 Aero のガラスはまだありません）。スタートメニュー、タスクバー、通知領域、フォルダの窓が動き、Linux のアプリもアプリごとに タスクバーにまとまります。確かめたのは日本語版と、その文字を英語の言語パックのものに入れ替えた英語版です。
- Windows XP Professional SP3（x86）の ISO（日本語版か英語版）をご自身で用意してください。
- 新しくインストールした Debian 13 に、ログインして使う利用者として入り、次を実行します。
git clone https://github.com/mikuta0407/win4wine.git ~/win4wine
cd ~/win4wine
scripts/setup.sh --media /path/to/WindowsXPSP3.iso --greeter
- 再起動すると、XP のようこそ画面からそのまま XP のシェルに入ります。
XP の ISO が無いときは、ReactOS のシェルで試せます（ISO は要りません）。
scripts/setup.sh --shell reactos --lang ja   # 英語なら --lang en
ログイン画面でセッション「ReactOS Shell (Wine, X11)」を選びます。
Windows 7 のシェル（実験的）は、Windows 7 SP1 x64 の ISO から作ります。
scripts/setup.sh --shell win7 --media /path/to/Windows7SP1x64.iso
ログイン画面でセッション「Windows 7 Shell (Wine, X11)」を選びます。
詳しい手順とオプションは 🔗 導入手順（INSTALL.md） にあります。Wine をソースからビルドするので、4 コアで 1 時間半ほどかかります。 テーマ（XP のクラシックなど）と文字の大きさは、後から変えられます（見た目と文字の大きさを変える）。
Warning
- Windows XP（と Windows 7）の ISO はご自身で入手してください。 このリポジトリにも導入スクリプトにも、Microsoft のファイルを 取得する処理はありません。ISO と取り出したファイルの扱いは、Microsoft の使用許諾に従ってください。
- 利用は自己責任でお願いします。 データの損失や不具合について、作者は責任を負いません。
- Microsoft とも Wine プロジェクトとも関係のない、非公式のプロジェクトです。
- 確かめているのは日本語版と英語版の XP SP3（x86）だけです。ほかの言語の XP も英語版と同じ設定で入れるので動くかもしれませんが、
一切試していません（setup.sh は警告を出して続けます）。
LightDM ─ XP のようこそ画面 (greeter/)
   └─ XP Shell セッション (session/supervisor.py)
        ├─ Wine 11.0 + 151 本のパッチ (wine/patches/)
        │    └─ XP の explorer.exe, shell32.dll, …（ISO から取り出したもの。ReactOS や Windows 7 のシェルではそれらのもの）
        ├─ bridge-wm: X11 のウィンドウマネージャー + Win32 の代理ウィンドウ (bridge-wm/)
        │    （Wayland のセッションでは、ウィンドウマネージャーの代わりにパッチを当てた labwc。wayland/）
        └─ fcitx5 + Mozc, トレイの仲介 (bridge-wm/tray.py)
仕組みや設計の詳しい説明は docs/ にまとめています。
- loss32: Wine の上の Win32 のソフトウェアだけでデスクトップを作る Linux ディストリビューションの計画です。 ReactOS の部品も使います。win4wine が XP 本物のシェルを Debian の上で動かすのに対し、loss32 は自由なソフトウェアだけで作ります。
- Windows 由来のバイナリには手を加えません。違いは Wine とその周辺で吸収します。
- Microsoft のバイナリを逆アセンブルしません。挙動は公開 API と、実際の XP の上で動かす自作のプローブで調べています。
- Microsoft のファイル、取り出したもの、Wine の prefix は、Git 管理外の localres/ と .local/ にだけ置きます。
- AshesTom さん（フォーク）: XP の画面のプロパティ、
サウンド、キーボードの配列、通知領域の XP のアイコン、スタートメニューの左の列、Internet Explorer 6 と Windows Media Player 9、
起動中のようこそ画面、タイトルバーのアニメーションと小さいアイコン、Arch Linux への対応、xp.sh など。
LGPL-2.1-or-later（Wine と同じ）です。対象はこのリポジトリのコードとパッチだけです。
ただし wayland/patches/labwc/ は labwc と同じ GPL-2.0-only、wayland/patches/wlroots/ は wlroots と同じ MIT です。
Windows XP と Windows 7 のファイルと、スクリーンショットに写っている XP と Windows 7 の画面の著作権は Microsoft にあります。
ReactOS のシェルで使う ReactOS のファイルは、ReactOS のライセンス（GPL-2.0-or-later、DLL の多くは LGPL-2.1-or-later）に従います。
